				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_idea = ABY_industrial_concern_neptis_group_4

							has_idea = ABY_industrial_concern_neptis_group_PMC
						}
					}
					remove_ideas = ABY_industrial_concern_neptis_group_4

							has_idea = ABY_industrial_concern_skoda_power
						}

					remove_ideas = ABY_industrial_concern_skoda_auto

					limit = {

							has_idea = ABY_industrial_concern_rothschild_3
							has_idea = ABY_industrial_concern_rothschild_america
							has_idea = ABY_industrial_concern_rothschild_swiss
						}
					}
